\lstdefinelanguage{Daeducer}{
  sensitive = true,
  keywords={reiteration, and_intro, and_elim_left, and_elim_right, imp_elim, imp_intro, bi_elim, bi_intro, or_intro_left, or_intro_right, or_elim, not_elim, not_intro, indirect, explosion, forall_intro, forall_elim, exists_intro, exists_elim, id_intro, id_elim},
  keywords = [2]{premise, assumption},
  keywords = [3]{!, ^, v, ->, <->, =},

  keywords = [5]{modus_tollens, double_not_elim, conv_not_exists, conv_forall_not, conv_not_forall, conv_exists_not, demorgan_not_or, demorgan_or_not, demorgan_not_and, demorgan_and_not, or_syllogism_left, or_syllogism_right, excluded_middle},

\begin{document}

\maketitle

This document presents proofs for all of the exercises and examples in the Forall-x: Calgary book, covering Truth Functional Logic and First Order Logic. The Modal Logic proofs are not covered.

All of these proofs were generated by the Qwen3.5-35B-A3B-UD model quantised to Q8\_K\_XL format with inference running on an Nvidia A100 or H100 GPU.

The proofs were generated and checked to be correct using a proof checking tool and have been transcribed directly into this document. Consequently although all of the proofs are correct and give the expected result, they may not be the most efficient or attractive solutions to the questions.

In some cases -- for example where a question asks for an equivalence to be proven -- the solutions may be split into multiple parts.

Each proof is presented using Fitch notation and is followed by the specific commands needed to enter the proof into Daeducer, the proof tool they were created with.

\section*{Proofs}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Example p.134}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {((P \land Q) \lor (P \land R))} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {(P \land Q)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {3} {P} \ae{2}
  \close
  %\have {4} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {5} {(P \land R)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {6} {P} \ae{5}
  \close
  %\have {7} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {8} {P} \oe{1,2-3,5-6}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise ((P ^ Q) v (P ^ R))
assumption (P ^ Q)
and_elim_left 2
discharge
assumption (P ^ R)
and_elim_left 5
discharge
or_elim 1, 2, 3, 5, 6
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Example p.134}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(A \land (B \lor C))} \by{PR}{}
  \have {2} {A} \ae{1}
  \have {3} {(B \lor C)} \ae{1}
  \open
  \hypo {4} {B} \by{AS}{}
  \have {5} {(A \land B)} \ai{2, 4}
  \have {6} {((A \land B) \lor (A \land C))} \oe{5}
  \close
  %\have {7} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {8} {C} \by{AS}{}
  \have {9} {(A \land C)} \ai{2, 8}
  \have {10} {((A \land B) \lor (A \land C))} \oe{9}
  \close
  %\have {11} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {12} {((A \land B) \lor (A \land C))} \oe{3,4-6,8-10}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (A ^ (B v C))
and_elim_left 1
and_elim_right 1
assumption B
and_intro 2, 4
or_intro_left 5, (A ^ C)
discharge
assumption C
and_intro 2, 8
or_intro_right 9, (A ^ B)
discharge
or_elim 3, 4, 6, 8, 10
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Example p.137}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {D} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {\neg D} \by{AS}{}
  \have {3} {\bot} \ne{2, 1}
  \close
  %\have {4} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {5} {\neg \neg D} \ni{2-3}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise D
assumption !D
not_elim 2, 1
discharge
not_intro 2, 3
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Example p.138}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {\neg \neg D} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {\neg D} \by{AS}{}
  \have {3} {\bot} \ne{1, 2}
  \close
  %\have {4} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {5} {D} \by{IP}{2-3}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise !!D
assumption !D
not_elim 1, 2
discharge
indirect 2, 3
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise A.1}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(A \land (B \land C))} \by{PR}{}
  \hypo {2} {((B \lor C) \to D)} \by{PR}{}
  \have {3} {(B \land C)} \ae{1}
  \have {4} {B} \ae{3}
  \have {5} {(B \lor C)} \oe{4}
  \have {6} {D} \by{$\to$E}{2, 5}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (A ^ (B ^ C))
premise ((B v C) -> D)
and_elim_right 1
and_elim_left 3
or_intro_left 4, C
imp_elim 2, 5
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise B.1}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(P \land S)} \by{PR}{}
  \hypo {2} {(S \to R)} \by{PR}{}
  \have {3} {S} \ae{1}
  \have {4} {R} \by{$\to$E}{2, 3}
  \have {5} {(R \lor E)} \oe{4}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (P ^ S)
premise (S -> R)
and_elim_right 1
imp_elim 2, 3
or_intro_left 4, E
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise B.2}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(A \to D)} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {(A \land B)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {3} {A} \ae{2}
  \have {4} {D} \by{$\to$E}{1, 3}
  \have {5} {(D \lor E)} \oe{4}
  \close
  %\have {6} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {7} {((A \land B) \to (D \lor E))} \by{$\to$I}{2,5}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (A -> D)
assumption (A ^ B)
and_elim_left 2
imp_elim 1, 3
or_intro_left 4, E
discharge
imp_intro 2, 5
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise B.3}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(\neg L \to (J \lor L))} \by{PR}{}
  \hypo {2} {\neg L} \by{PR}{}
  \have {3} {(J \lor L)} \by{$\to$E}{1, 2}
  \open
  \hypo {4} {J} \by{AS}{}
  \have {5} {J} \r{4}
  \close
  %\have {6} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {7} {L} \by{AS}{}
  \have {8} {\bot} \ne{2, 7}
  \have {9} {J} \by{X}{8}
  \close
  %\have {10} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {11} {J} \oe{3,4-5,7-9}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (!L -> (J v L))
premise !L
imp_elim 1, 2
assumption J
reiteration 4
discharge
assumption L
not_elim 2, 7
explosion 8, J
discharge
or_elim 3, 4, 5, 7, 9
qed
\end{lstlisting}

  \have {3} {(Q \land \neg Q)} \by{$\to$E}{1, 2}

  \have {5} {\bot} \ne{4, 2}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise C.3}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(A \to (B \to C))} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {(A \land B)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {3} {A} \ae{2}
  \have {4} {B} \ae{2}
  \have {5} {(B \to C)} \by{$\to$E}{1, 3}
  \have {6} {C} \by{$\to$E}{5, 4}

  \have {8} {((A \land B) \to C)} \by{$\to$I}{2,6}

premise (A -> (B -> C))
assumption (A ^ B)
and_elim_left 2
and_elim_right 2
imp_elim 1, 3

discharge
imp_intro 2, 6
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise C.4}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(K \land L)} \by{PR}{}
  \have {2} {K} \ae{1}
  \have {3} {L} \ae{1}
  \open
  \hypo {4} {K} \by{AS}{}
  \have {5} {L} \r{3}
  \close
  %\have {6} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {7} {(K \to L)} \by{$\to$I}{4,5}
  \open
  \hypo {8} {L} \by{AS}{}
  \have {9} {K} \r{2}
  \close
  %\have {10} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {11} {(L \to K)} \by{$\to$I}{8,9}
  \have {12} {((K \to L) \land (L \to K))} \ai{7, 11}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (K ^ L)
and_elim_left 1
and_elim_right 1
assumption K
reiteration 3
discharge
imp_intro 4, 5
assumption L
reiteration 2
discharge
imp_intro 8, 9
and_intro 7, 11
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise C.5}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {((C \land D) \lor E)} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {(C \land D)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {3} {D} \ae{2}
  \have {4} {(E \lor D)} \oe{3}
  \close
  %\have {5} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {6} {E} \by{AS}{}
  \have {7} {(E \lor D)} \oe{6}
  \close
  %\have {8} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {9} {(E \lor D)} \oe{1,2-4,6-7}

\end{nd}

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

premise ((C ^ D) v E)
assumption (C ^ D)

or_intro_right 3, E
discharge

or_elim 1, 2, 4, 6, 7

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise C.6}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {((A \to B) \land (B \to A))} \by{PR}{}
  \hypo {2} {((B \to C) \land (C \to B))} \by{PR}{}
  \have {3} {(A \to B)} \ae{1}
  \have {4} {(B \to C)} \ae{2}
  \open
  \hypo {5} {A} \by{AS}{}
  \have {6} {B} \by{$\to$E}{3, 5}
  \have {7} {C} \by{$\to$E}{4, 6}
  \close
  %\have {8} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {9} {(A \to C)} \by{$\to$I}{5,7}
  \have {10} {(B \to A)} \ae{1}
  \have {11} {(C \to B)} \ae{2}
  \open
  \hypo {12} {C} \by{AS}{}
  \have {13} {B} \by{$\to$E}{11, 12}
  \have {14} {A} \by{$\to$E}{10, 13}
  \close
  %\have {15} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {16} {(C \to A)} \by{$\to$I}{12,14}
  \have {17} {((A \to C) \land (C \to A))} \ai{9, 16}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise ((A -> B) ^ (B -> A))
premise ((B -> C) ^ (C -> B))
and_elim_left 1
and_elim_left 2
assumption A
imp_elim 3, 5
imp_elim 4, 6
discharge
imp_intro 5, 7
and_elim_right 1
and_elim_right 2
assumption C
imp_elim 11, 12
imp_elim 10, 13
discharge
imp_intro 12, 14
and_intro 9, 16
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise C.7}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(\neg F \to G)} \by{PR}{}
  \hypo {2} {(F \to H)} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {3} {\neg (G \lor H)} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {4} {F} \by{AS}{}
  \have {5} {H} \by{$\to$E}{2, 4}
  \have {6} {(G \lor H)} \oe{5}
  \have {7} {\bot} \ne{3, 6}
  \close
  %\have {8} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {9} {\neg F} \ni{4-7}
  \have {10} {G} \by{$\to$E}{1, 9}
  \have {11} {(G \lor H)} \oe{10}
  \have {12} {\bot} \ne{3, 11}
  \close
  %\have {13} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {14} {(G \lor H)} \by{IP}{3-12}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (!F -> G)
premise (F -> H)
assumption !(G v H)
assumption F
imp_elim 2, 4
or_intro_right 5, G
not_elim 3, 6
discharge
not_intro 4, 7
imp_elim 1, 9
or_intro_left 10, H
not_elim 3, 11
discharge
indirect 3, 12
qed
\end{lstlisting}

  \open
  \hypo {3} {(Z \land K)} \by{AS}{}

  \close
  %\have {6} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {7} {(K \land M)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {8} {K} \ae{7}
  \have {9} {D} \by{$\to$E}{2, 8}
  \close
  %\have {10} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {11} {D} \oe{1,3-5,7-9}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise ((Z ^ K) v (K ^ M))
premise (K -> D)
assumption (Z ^ K)
and_elim_right 3
imp_elim 2, 4
discharge
assumption (K ^ M)
and_elim_left 7
imp_elim 2, 8
discharge
or_elim 1, 3, 5, 7, 9
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise C.9}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(P \land (Q \lor R))} \by{PR}{}
  \hypo {2} {(P \to \neg R)} \by{PR}{}
  \have {3} {P} \ae{1}
  \have {4} {(Q \lor R)} \ae{1}
  \have {5} {\neg R} \by{$\to$E}{2, 3}
  \open
  \hypo {6} {Q} \by{AS}{}
  \have {7} {(Q \lor E)} \oe{6}
  \close
  %\have {8} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {9} {R} \by{AS}{}
  \have {10} {\bot} \ne{5, 9}
  \have {11} {(Q \lor E)} \by{X}{10}
  \close
  %\have {12} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {13} {(Q \lor E)} \oe{4,6-7,9-11}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (P ^ (Q v R))
premise (P -> !R)
and_elim_left 1
and_elim_right 1
imp_elim 2, 3
assumption Q
or_intro_left 6, E
discharge
assumption R
not_elim 5, 9
explosion 10, (Q v E)
discharge
or_elim 4, 6, 7, 9, 11
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise C.10}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {((S \to T) \land (T \to S))} \by{PR}{}
  \have {2} {(S \to T)} \ae{1}
  \have {3} {(T \to S)} \ae{1}
  \open
  \hypo {4} {S} \by{AS}{}
  \have {5} {(T \lor S)} \oe{4}
  \close
  %\have {6} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {7} {(S \to (T \lor S))} \by{$\to$I}{4,5}
  \open
  \hypo {8} {(T \lor S)} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {9} {T} \by{AS}{}
  \have {10} {S} \by{$\to$E}{3, 9}
  \close
  %\have {11} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {12} {S} \by{AS}{}
  \have {13} {S} \r{12}
  \close
  %\have {14} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {15} {S} \oe{8,9-10,12-13}
  \close
  %\have {16} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {17} {((T \lor S) \to S)} \by{$\to$I}{8,15}
  \have {18} {((S \to (T \lor S)) \land ((T \lor S) \to S))} \ai{7, 17}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise ((S -> T) ^ (T -> S))
and_elim_left 1
and_elim_right 1
assumption S
or_intro_right 4, T
discharge
imp_intro 4, 5
assumption (T v S)
assumption T
imp_elim 3, 9
discharge
assumption S
reiteration 12
discharge
or_elim 8, 9, 10, 12, 13
discharge
imp_intro 8, 15
and_intro 7, 17
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 17, Exercise C.11}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {\neg (P \to Q)} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {Q} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {3} {P} \by{AS}{}
  \have {4} {Q} \r{2}
  \close
  %\have {5} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {6} {(P \to Q)} \by{$\to$I}{3,4}
  \have {7} {\bot} \ne{1, 6}

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(\neg A \to \neg B)} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {B} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {3} {\neg A} \by{AS}{}
  \have {4} {\neg B} \by{$\to$E}{1, 3}
  \have {5} {\bot} \ne{4, 2}
  \close
  %\have {6} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {7} {A} \by{IP}{3-5}
  \close
  %\have {8} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {9} {(B \to A)} \by{$\to$I}{2,7}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (!A -> !B)
assumption B
assumption !A
imp_elim 1, 3
not_elim 4, 2
discharge
indirect 3, 5
discharge
imp_intro 2, 7
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 18, Exercise D.5}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

\begin{fitchproof}[arrayenv=longtable]
  \hypo {1} {(A \to (B \lor C))} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {\neg ((A \to B) \lor (A \to C))} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {3} {(A \to B)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {4} {((A \to B) \lor (A \to C))} \oe{3}
  \have {5} {\bot} \ne{2, 4}
  \close
  %\have {6} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {7} {\neg (A \to B)} \ni{3-5}
  \open
  \hypo {8} {(A \to C)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {9} {((A \to B) \lor (A \to C))} \oe{8}
  \have {10} {\bot} \ne{2, 9}
  \close
  %\have {11} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {12} {\neg (A \to C)} \ni{8-10}
  \open
  \hypo {13} {\neg A} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {14} {A} \by{AS}{}
  \have {15} {\bot} \ne{13, 14}
  \have {16} {B} \by{X}{15}
  \close
  %\have {17} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {18} {(A \to B)} \by{$\to$I}{14,16}
  \have {19} {\bot} \ne{7, 18}
  \close
  %\have {20} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {21} {A} \by{IP}{13-19}
  \have {22} {(B \lor C)} \by{$\to$E}{1, 21}
  \open
  \hypo {23} {B} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {24} {A} \by{AS}{}
  \have {25} {B} \r{23}
  \close
  %\have {26} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {27} {(A \to B)} \by{$\to$I}{24,25}
  \have {28} {\bot} \ne{7, 27}
  \close
  %\have {29} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {30} {\neg B} \ni{23-28}
  \open
  \hypo {31} {C} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {32} {A} \by{AS}{}
  \have {33} {C} \r{31}
  \close
  %\have {34} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {35} {(A \to C)} \by{$\to$I}{32,33}
  \have {36} {\bot} \ne{12, 35}
  \close
  %\have {37} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {38} {\neg C} \ni{31-36}
  \have {39} {\bot} \oe{22,23-28,31-36}
  \close
  %\have {40} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {41} {((A \to B) \lor (A \to C))} \by{IP}{2-39}
\end{fitchproof}

  \hypo {1} {(M \land (\neg N \to \neg M))} \by{PR}{}
  \have {2} {M} \ae{1}
  \have {3} {(\neg N \to \neg M)} \ae{1}
  \open
  \hypo {4} {\neg N} \by{AS}{}
  \have {5} {\neg M} \by{$\to$E}{3, 4}
  \have {6} {\bot} \ne{5, 2}
  \close
  %\have {7} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {8} {N} \by{IP}{4-6}
  \have {9} {(N \land M)} \ai{8, 2}
  \have {10} {((N \land M) \lor \neg M)} \oe{9}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (M ^ (!N -> !M))
and_elim_left 1
and_elim_right 1
assumption !N
imp_elim 3, 4
not_elim 5, 2
discharge
indirect 4, 6
and_intro 8, 2
or_intro_left 9, !M
qed
\end{lstlisting}

  \hypo {12} {K} \by{AS}{}

  \have {14} {R} \by{X}{13}
  \close

  \open

  \have {30} {(\neg (F \land (K \land R)) \land ((F \to ((K \to R) \land (R \to K))) \land (((K \to R) \land (R \to K)) \to F)))} \ai{10, 29}

  \hypo {18} {A(d)} \by{AS}{}

id_intro n
and_intro 1, 2
exists_intro 3, ((F(x) ^ forall y (F(y) -> x = y)) ^ x = n)
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 39, Exercise C.1.a}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {(\exists x F(x) \land \forall x \forall y ((F(x) \land F(y)) \to x = y))} \by{PR}{}
  \have {2} {\exists x F(x)} \ae{1}
  \have {3} {\forall x \forall y ((F(x) \land F(y)) \to x = y)} \ae{1}
  \open
  \hypo {4} {F(a)} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {5} {F(z)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {6} {\forall y ((F(a) \land F(y)) \to a = y)} \Ae{3}
  \have {7} {((F(a) \land F(z)) \to a = z)} \Ae{6}
  \have {8} {(F(a) \land F(z))} \ai{4, 5}
  \have {9} {a = z} \by{$\to$E}{7, 8}
  \close
  %\have {10} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {11} {(F(z) \to a = z)} \by{$\to$I}{5,9}
  \have {12} {\forall y (F(y) \to a = y)} \Ai{11}
  \have {13} {(F(a) \land \forall y (F(y) \to a = y))} \ai{4, 12}
  \have {14} {\exists x (F(x) \land \forall y (F(y) \to x = y))} \Ei{13}
  \close
  %\have {15} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {16} {\exists x (F(x) \land \forall y (F(y) \to x = y))} \Ee{2,4-14}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise (exists x F(x) ^ forall x forall y ((F(x) ^ F(y)) -> x = y))
and_elim_left 1
and_elim_right 1
assumption F(a)
assumption F(z)
forall_elim 3, a
forall_elim 6, z
and_intro 4, 5
imp_elim 7, 8
discharge
imp_intro 5, 9
forall_intro 11, z, y
and_intro 4, 12
exists_intro 13, (F(x) ^ forall y (F(y) -> x = y))
discharge
exists_elim 2, 4, 14
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 39, Exercise C.1.b}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {\exists x (F(x) \land \forall y (F(y) \to x = y))} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {(F(a) \land \forall y (F(y) \to a = y))} \by{AS}{}
  \have {3} {F(a)} \ae{2}
  \have {4} {\forall y (F(y) \to a = y)} \ae{2}
  \open
  \hypo {5} {F(z)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {6} {(F(z) \to a = z)} \Ae{4}
  \have {7} {a = z} \by{$\to$E}{6, 5}
  \close
  %\have {8} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {9} {a = z} \by{AS}{}
  \have {10} {F(z)} \by{=E}{9,3}
  \close
  %\have {11} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {12} {(F(z) \leftrightarrow a = z)} \by{$\leftrightarrow$I}{5-7,9-10}
  \have {13} {\forall y (F(y) \leftrightarrow a = y)} \Ai{12}
  \have {14} {\exists x \forall y (F(y) \leftrightarrow x = y)} \Ei{13}
  \close
  %\have {15} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {16} {\exists x \forall y (F(y) \leftrightarrow x = y)} \Ee{1,2-14}

\end{nd}
$

assumption F(z)

discharge

discharge
bi_intro 5, 7, 9, 10

discharge

qed

\subsection*{Chapter 39, Exercise C.1.c}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {\exists x \forall y (F(y) \leftrightarrow x = y)} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {\forall y (F(y) \leftrightarrow c = y)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {3} {(F(c) \leftrightarrow c = c)} \Ae{2}
  \have {4} {c = c} \by{=I}{}
  \have {5} {F(c)} \by{$\leftrightarrow$E}{3,4}
  \have {6} {\exists x F(x)} \Ei{5}
  \open
  \hypo {7} {(F(a) \land F(b))} \by{AS}{}
  \have {8} {F(a)} \ae{7}
  \have {9} {F(b)} \ae{7}
  \have {10} {(F(a) \leftrightarrow c = a)} \Ae{2}
  \have {11} {c = a} \by{$\leftrightarrow$E}{10,8}
  \have {12} {(F(b) \leftrightarrow c = b)} \Ae{2}
  \have {13} {c = b} \by{$\leftrightarrow$E}{12,9}
  \have {14} {a = b} \by{=E}{11,13}
  \close
  %\have {15} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {16} {((F(a) \land F(b)) \to a = b)} \by{$\to$I}{7,14}
  \have {17} {\forall y ((F(a) \land F(y)) \to a = y)} \Ai{16}
  \have {18} {\forall x \forall y ((F(x) \land F(y)) \to x = y)} \Ai{17}
  \have {19} {(\exists x F(x) \land \forall x \forall y ((F(x) \land F(y)) \to x = y))} \ai{6, 18}
  \close
  %\have {20} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {21} {(\exists x F(x) \land \forall x \forall y ((F(x) \land F(y)) \to x = y))} \Ee{1,2-19}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise exists x forall y (F(y) <-> x = y)
assumption forall y (F(y) <-> c = y)
forall_elim 2, c
id_intro c
bi_elim 3, 4
exists_intro 5, F(x)
assumption (F(a) ^ F(b))
and_elim_left 7
and_elim_right 7
forall_elim 2, a
bi_elim 10, 8
forall_elim 2, b
bi_elim 12, 9
id_elim 11, 13, a = b
discharge
imp_intro 7, 14
forall_intro 16, b, y
forall_intro 17, a, x
and_intro 6, 18
discharge
exists_elim 1, 2, 19
qed
\end{lstlisting}

\subsection*{Chapter 39, Exercise D.1}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

\begin{fitchproof}[arrayenv=longtable]
  \hypo {1} {\exists x (F(x) \land \forall y (F(y) \to x = y))} \by{PR}{}
  \hypo {2} {\exists x (G(x) \land \forall y (G(y) \to x = y))} \by{PR}{}
  \hypo {3} {\forall x \neg (F(x) \land G(x))} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {4} {(F(a) \land \forall y (F(y) \to a = y))} \by{AS}{}
  \have {5} {F(a)} \ae{4}
  \have {6} {\forall y (F(y) \to a = y)} \ae{4}
  \open
  \hypo {7} {(G(b) \land \forall y (G(y) \to b = y))} \by{AS}{}
  \have {8} {G(b)} \ae{7}
  \have {9} {\forall y (G(y) \to b = y)} \ae{7}
  \open
  \hypo {10} {a = b} \by{AS}{}
  \have {11} {\forall x \neg (F(x) \land G(x))} \r{3}
  \have {12} {\neg (F(a) \land G(a))} \Ae{11}
  \have {13} {G(a)} \by{=E}{10,8}
  \have {14} {(F(a) \land G(a))} \ai{5, 13}
  \have {15} {\bot} \ne{12, 14}
  \close
  %\have {16} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {17} {\neg a = b} \ni{10-15}
  \have {18} {(F(a) \lor G(a))} \oe{5}
  \have {19} {(F(b) \lor G(b))} \oe{8}
  \open
  \hypo {20} {(F(z) \lor G(z))} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {21} {F(z)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {22} {(F(z) \to a = z)} \Ae{6}
  \have {23} {a = z} \by{$\to$E}{22, 21}
  \have {24} {a = a} \by{=I}{}
  \have {25} {z = a} \by{=E}{23,24}
  \have {26} {(z = a \lor z = b)} \oe{25}
  \close
  %\have {27} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {28} {G(z)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {29} {(G(z) \to b = z)} \Ae{9}
  \have {30} {b = z} \by{$\to$E}{29, 28}
  \have {31} {b = b} \by{=I}{}
  \have {32} {z = b} \by{=E}{30,31}
  \have {33} {(z = a \lor z = b)} \oe{32}
  \close
  %\have {34} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {35} {(z = a \lor z = b)} \oe{20,21-26,28-33}
  \close
  %\have {36} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {37} {((F(z) \lor G(z)) \to (z = a \lor z = b))} \by{$\to$I}{20,35}
  \have {38} {\forall z ((F(z) \lor G(z)) \to (z = a \lor z = b))} \Ai{37}
  \have {39} {((F(b) \lor G(b)) \land \forall z ((F(z) \lor G(z)) \to (z = a \lor z = b)))} \ai{19, 38}
  \have {40} {((F(a) \lor G(a)) \land ((F(b) \lor G(b)) \land \forall z ((F(z) \lor G(z)) \\ \quad \to (z = a \lor z = b))))} \ai{18, 39}
  \have {41} {(\neg a = b \land ((F(a) \lor G(a)) \land ((F(b) \lor G(b)) \land \forall z ((F(z) \lor G(z)) \\ \quad \to (z = a \lor z = b)))))} \ai{17, 40}
  \have {42} {\exists y (\neg a = y \land ((F(a) \lor G(a)) \land ((F(y) \lor G(y)) \land \forall z ((F(z) \lor G(z)) \\ \quad \to (z = a \lor z = y)))))} \Ei{41}
  \have {43} {\exists x \exists y (\neg x = y \land ((F(x) \lor G(x)) \land ((F(y) \lor G(y)) \land \forall z ((F(z) \lor G(z)) \\ \quad \to (z = x \lor z = y)))))} \Ei{42}
  \close
  %\have {44} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {45} {\exists x \exists y (\neg x = y \land ((F(x) \lor G(x)) \land ((F(y) \lor G(y)) \land \forall z ((F(z) \lor G(z)) \\ \quad \to (z = x \lor z = y)))))} \Ee{2,7-43}
  \close
  %\have {46} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {47} {\exists x \exists y (\neg x = y \land ((F(x) \lor G(x)) \land ((F(y) \lor G(y)) \land \forall z ((F(z) \lor G(z)) \\ \quad \to (z = x \lor z = y)))))} \Ee{1,4-45}
  \have {48} {\exists x \exists y (\neg x = y \land ((F(x) \lor G(x)) \land ((F(y) \lor G(y)) \land \forall z ((F(z) \lor G(z)) \\ \quad \to (z = x \lor z = y)))))} \Ee{1,4-45}
\end{fitchproof}

\subsection*{Chapter 40, Example p.330}

Proof presented using Fitch notation:

$
\begin{nd}
  \hypo {1} {\forall x \neg A(x)} \by{PR}{}
  \open
  \hypo {2} {\exists x A(x)} \by{AS}{}
  \open
  \hypo {3} {A(c)} \by{AS}{}
  \have {4} {\neg A(c)} \Ae{1}
  \have {5} {\bot} \ne{4, 3}
  \close
  %\have {6} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {7} {\bot} \Ee{2,3-5}
  \close
  %\have {8} {} \by{DIS}{}
  \have {9} {\neg \exists x A(x)} \ni{2-7}

\end{nd}
$

Daeducer commands needed to generate the proof:

\begin{lstlisting}[language=Daeducer]
premise forall x !A(x)
assumption exists x A(x)
assumption A(c)
forall_elim 1, c
not_elim 4, 3
discharge
exists_elim 2, 3, 5
discharge
not_intro 2, 7
qed
\end{lstlisting}
